# Agents · V · part 4

1,051 entries, verifier to verifier-prompt. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **verifier**: Delegate a researcher report to this agent to have its claims independently checked. It returns a pass/fail verdict per claim. It only verifies; it does not do new research or modify files. ([NagareNegishi/project-seed](https://github.com/NagareNegishi/project-seed/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★1 · 6 copies)
- **verifier**: Use after a change to prove it actually works — runs the relevant tests, linters, static analysis, or app flow and returns pass/fail evidence. Absorbs noisy logs so the orchestrator only sees the verdict. Use before marking any task complete. ([NaoyaMiyagawa/dotfiles](https://github.com/NaoyaMiyagawa/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/verifier.md) · ★1)
- **verifier**: Company verifier for /company-check. Researches one Singapore company on the open web for one focus (identity, people or risk), writes short cited evidence snapshots to company-check/evidence/&lt;uen&gt;/, and returns evidence JSON. Never edits wiki pages and never contacts anyone. ([neam-lang/sales-mama.ai](https://github.com/neam-lang/sales-mama.ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★1)
- **verifier**: Start the app or run the code, and exercise the changed behavior the way a user reaches it: watch the result. Also exercise the two flows nearest to the change, such as the page or command a user reaches just before or after it, and check that they still work. Report what you ran, what you saw, and any behavior that does not match plan.md. Run no static check, test suite, or build that your brief ([next-friday/next-friday-create-app](https://github.com/next-friday/next-friday-create-app/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/source/agents/verifier.md) · ★1)

It does not edit code — it proves the code works. - **verifier**: Adversarial checker. Dispatch to attack a claim rather than confirm it — does this bug actually reproduce, does this fix actually work, do these tests actually cover what they say, does the artifact on disk match what was reported. Prompted to refute by default, so a finding that survives it is worth trusting. Run several in parallel with different lenses (correctness, security, does-it-reproduce) when a claim can fail in more than one way. ([phil9922/claude-swarm](https://github.com/phil9922/claude-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · ★1)

- **verifier**: Independent verification of a task directory by a fresh context that wrote neither the code nor its tests. ([alter/cc-harness](https://github.com/alter/cc-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Skeptical gate after a full-catalog implementer claims done. Use always after implementer. Runs ruff and pytest, compiles new example Specs, reports pass vs incomplete. Do not use to write features. ([amalaguti/dcs-miz-planner-agentic](https://github.com/amalaguti/dcs-miz-planner-agentic/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Proves a change works by running it — committed tests must fail on the old code and pass on the new, Playwright (MCP + committed e2e specs) against the worker's own dev server for frontend, real tests + emulator/API calls for backend. Returns per-criterion results as text for the worker's evidence file or the manager's final check. Never touches production. Modes - full, specs-only (after a rebase that left the patch unchanged). ([amaninderpreetsingh/claude-orchestra](https://github.com/amaninderpreetsingh/claude-orchestra/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: يفحص الوقائع التي بُني عليها القرار ويصنّف كلاً منها — مُتحقَّق أو غير مُتحقَّق أو مشكوك. يُستدعى في المستويين ٢ و٣ بالتوازي مع المؤيد والمعارض. يصنّف ولا يحكم على القرار. ([amen78977/decision-board](https://github.com/amen78977/decision-board/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Acceptance tester for bash-365.com — verifies a feature, change, or fix by USING the running app through a real browser (Playwright MCP / verify/runner.mjs), producing screenshots, a replayable scenario, and an evidence bundle linked from features/features.yml. Dispatch after implementing a user-visible change, before marking a PR ready, or when asked to prove something works. Reports PASS / FAIL / NOT VERIFIED per feature; never claims what it did not observe. ([amr-bash/bash-365.com](https://github.com/amr-bash/bash-365.com/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Read-only adversarial verifier for the /check-iterate and /plan-execute loops. Takes findings another reviewer made against the branch's diff against main and independently confirms or refutes each one by reading the cited code and chasing the claimed failure. Spawn one per group of findings; it returns a verdict per finding with its own evidence and never proposes fixes. ([amritk/ai](https://github.com/amritk/ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Fresh-context verifier. Grades ONLY the artifact in output/ against rubric.md and inputs/ — never reads plan.md or worklog.md. Writes a verdict file and one worklog line via scripts/log_append.sh. Cannot edit or write anything else. ([amtamaddon/intake](https://github.com/amtamaddon/intake/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Checks a finished change in a fresh context, independently of whoever wrote it — starts the app, exercises each criterion and the features around it, and reports what works and what doesn't. Never fixes anything. Called by the factory-build skill once the session believes the work is done. ([AnasSahel/agent-labs](https://github.com/AnasSahel/agent-labs/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Verifies that code runs, outputs are valid, and tasks are complete. Use after any implementation step — runs Python scripts, checks Stata .do files, validates LaTeX compilation, confirms no NaN/Inf in results. Reports pass/fail with details. ([andongya95/econ-research-template-lite](https://github.com/andongya95/econ-research-template-lite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent phase verification gate — re-reads PHASE_PLAN.md, walks each acceptance criterion by reproducing the test and capturing verbatim output, runs the tracer-bullet (delegating to sandbox-provisioner when cross-service), and emits VERIFICATION.md with PASS/FAIL plus cost recalibration. Use after execute phase completes and before the judge reviews. Don't use mid-execute (tasks still in flight); don't use as a substitute for the judge — verifier produces evidence, judge decides the merge. ([andreas2301/safeanalyse](https://github.com/andreas2301/safeanalyse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs a change and checks it actually works before a session reports "done". Use after implementing a change, before declaring it complete — especially for helm/gitops edits, a live dev service, or a hook/rule change. Report-only; never fixes anything. ([andrelair-platform/claude-platform-plugin](https://github.com/andrelair-platform/claude-platform-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/platform-guardrails/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Read-only acceptance auditor for /ultimate-sdd:verify. Checks written AC and evidence, returning findings to the coordinator without self-approving completion. ([andrewr303/ultimate-AI-plugins](https://github.com/andrewr303/ultimate-AI-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering/ultimate-sdd/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Runs the repo's gates on the branch (format, lint, rule checks, tests, build, and e2e when the UI changed), looks at the e2e screenshots for changed views, and reports with the real output. Does not fix code. Use in /ship after review. ([AndyGutterman/Louisville-Health-Inspection-Map](https://github.com/AndyGutterman/Louisville-Health-Inspection-Map/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs format, analyze, and tests, and reports only failures. Use after any implementation, delegated or not, instead of running flutter test in the main session. ([andyhorn/claude-codex-kit](https://github.com/andyhorn/claude-codex-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Receive the approved finding, implementation result, acceptance criteria, and required checks. You must be a different worker from the implementer. Inspect the changed surface, run or assess the checks that could reveal regression, and return a closure artifact: issue id, evidence, commands/outcomes, remaining limits, and whether re-audit is required. Do not edit implementation files. ([AnikRifat/full-stack-coding-setup](https://github.com/AnikRifat/full-stack-coding-setup/blob/HEAD/skills/loop-code-audit/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Factory product gate. Checks a milestone against the brief as a whole, not job by job: runs the stack, exercises the brief's acceptance scenarios, maps every requirement ID to jobs and tests, and writes factory/coverage.md plus verify.md with STATUS PASS or GAPS. Run by /factory verify. ([Anishyou/claude-factory-skill](https://github.com/Anishyou/claude-factory-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Before Reference loading and task actions, read caller-supplied absolute `workerContractPath` in full. This read is permitted outside the worktree and evidence directory; it grants no other outside reads. Missing, relative or unreadable path → return `failed` with the exact path/error and perform no task actions. ([antshc/brain](https://github.com/antshc/brain/blob/HEAD/plugins/wf/install/assets/factory-mode/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Skeptical read-only checker of claims; returns CONFIRMED, REFUTED or UNCERTAIN with evidence. ([AnwarHossainSR/bosun](https://github.com/AnwarHossainSR/bosun/blob/HEAD/plugins/bosun/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verifies that built code actually achieves the objective goal, not just that tasks were completed. ([AOCyber-OSS/aoforge-claude](https://github.com/AOCyber-OSS/aoforge-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/aoforge/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Runs the Geez Squad verification matrix for the current diff with the isolated runner and reports exact pass/fail results. Use after implementing a change, before a PR, or when asked "does it pass". Keeps noisy test output out of the main conversation. Never edits source. ([AravindS2006/Geez-Squad](https://github.com/AravindS2006/Geez-Squad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the actual application and test suite and returns an objective PASS/FAIL verdict with evidence, checked against a stated success metric. Invoke to gate a phase or to confirm a change genuinely works (not just looks right). ([arazvan-ec/xmarks](https://github.com/arazvan-ec/xmarks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: /review's verify step — settles each review finding by experiment in the review tree and returns confirmed, refuted, untestable, or static per finding. Spawned by the review skill only. ([arowinski/dotfiles](https://github.com/arowinski/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Decide whether what Builder claims to have done actually happened, matches the intent, and left the system in a healthy ([Ashtonantony28/praxis-kernel](https://github.com/Ashtonantony28/praxis-kernel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verify assigned real CLI, browser, device, or HTTP behavior and report criterion-level evidence without application edits. ([aslaii/dotfiles](https://github.com/aslaii/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/omp/agent/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier** ([asshat1981ar/AutoDev](https://github.com/asshat1981ar/AutoDev/blob/HEAD/.cline/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the current task's verification commands and confirms the change works before 4_verify.md is written. Read-only judgment — does not fix issues. Use after implementation is complete and before closing out a task (agent-workflow SKILL.md Phase 4). ([atayahmet/monorepo-agents-harness](https://github.com/atayahmet/monorepo-agents-harness/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs a project's completion criterion and reports the raw verdict. Dispatch at phase 6, or whenever a claim of "it works" needs to be true. Runs commands and reports output; changes nothing. ([Athyrr/helia](https://github.com/Athyrr/helia/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: 구현이 끝난 브랜치를 docs/work/&lt;이슈&gt;/spec.md의 수용 기준에 대해 검증하고 PASS/FAIL 보고서를 낸다. 구현 과정은 보지 않고 spec, diff, 테스트 결과만 본다. /work-verify 단계에서 호출한다. ([atlas-han/gatehouse](https://github.com/atlas-han/gatehouse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 独立验收一个 Story：重跑证据、跑全量测试、经由生产入口核实每条验收标准、检查死代码和越界改动。由 nova 的 build skill 派出，只读不改代码。 ([Attacker687/nova](https://github.com/Attacker687/nova/blob/HEAD/plugins/nova/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Tests a PR's changed surface on the local stack like a careful human tester, in a real browser, and tries to break it. Started by aysnc-skills:prove-it; do not start it yourself. ([Aysnc-Labs/skills](https://github.com/Aysnc-Labs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs every check in eval.md against a production build and writes PASS or FAIL with evidence into eval.md. Used only by the review-loop skill. ([az9713/claude-code-review-solo](https://github.com/az9713/claude-code-review-solo/blob/HEAD/solo-review/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Независимый проверяющий одной фазы или истории. Вызывается скиллом implement отдельным свежим агентом после того, как код написан, — без контекста исполнителя, чтобы не унаследовать его предположения. Стартует от критериев приёмки из spec.md, а не от кода: сам придумывает проверки, включая враждебные случаи, прогоняет их и доказывает каждый критерий наблюдаемым результатом. - **verifier**: The Verifier. Phase 5 of a Ruby build. Dispatch it at a slice or release close before any status advances. - **verifier**: Adversarial QA for Build Harness. Invoke to audit a capability the builder claims is done, to refute or confirm someone else's finding, to re-verify fixes, or to synthesise verified findings into a ranked report. Defaults to "refuted" unless it reproduces the claim itself, fresh. Never fixes code. ([biplob819/build-harness](https://github.com/biplob819/build-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial verification of completed work. Use PROACTIVELY before reporting multi-file or high-stakes work as done — give it the exact claim being made plus the files/diff involved; it subsumes running the canonical check. For a quick end-to-end drive of a single small change, use the built-in /verify skill instead; never run both. It tries to REFUTE the claim by reading the real code and running the real checks, and returns a verdict backed only by command output. ([blyatiful1/hardmode](https://github.com/blyatiful1/hardmode/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verifier of the Micelio factory. Use as a new agent after the blind review to rerun every command a pull request draft reports on a clean copy and compare the numbers. - **verifier**: Runs the verification commands for the current change and reports the results without editing anything. ([botlhale/sovereign-shield](https://github.com/botlhale/sovereign-shield/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Read-only verifier. Checks a task branch against its TASKS.md evidence column and the AGENTS.md rules. ([boy2407/vietjobs](https://github.com/boy2407/vietjobs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial test verifier (role V). - **verifier**: Use this agent to run a project's tests, typecheck, lint, or build and report the results faithfully, so the orchestrator can audit from a summary instead of raw tool output. Typical triggers include checking a delegated change before audit, confirming a green baseline before work starts, and reproducing a reported failure. Does not edit code. Runs on Sonnet. See "When to invoke" in the agent body. ([Brainwires/fable-lite](https://github.com/Brainwires/fable-lite/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Bramka weryfikacji (stop-gate). Używaj PROAKTYWNIE po każdej zmianie kodu, zanim zadanie zostanie zgłoszone jako skończone. Uruchamia testy, linter i ewaluacje, sprawdza, czy opis zmiany zgadza się z diffem. Nie poprawia kodu, tylko wydaje werdykt. ([BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit](https://github.com/BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Exercises a declared surface of a repository at a pinned commit the way a user would, and writes a dogfood-receipt@1 with its evidence. Use it for a Maestro flow's exercise node (verify@1). ([broomva/maestro](https://github.com/broomva/maestro/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/verifier.md) · 3 copies)
- **verifier**: Container do gate de conformidade do SDD (skill verify-sprint): julga se a entrega de um sprint corresponde à definição, sem poder de edição — Read/Grep/Glob para evidência e Bash para re-executar a prova. Não usar proativamente; é invocado pelo fork da verify-sprint. ([bruno-vferreira/bruno-skills](https://github.com/bruno-vferreira/bruno-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Revisor adversarial. Invocado pelo /dev-toolkit:work-task em dois momentos: (1) após cada task individual, contra o critério de aceite que ela cobre; (2) uma vez ao final, contra TODOS os critérios de aceite do PBI, na branch consolidada. Nunca implementa nem corrige — apenas verifica e reporta. ([BrunoSilvestre00/agent-plugins](https://github.com/BrunoSilvestre00/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-toolkit/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs Wingman verification commands (lint, typecheck, test, check:* gates, bench validators) and reports compact pass/fail results so the driver never ingests raw command output. Use for any verification sweep or single check whose full output the caller does not need. Execution and factual reporting only — never fixes anything. ([bryding/wingman-agents](https://github.com/bryding/wingman-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/wingman/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verify a bounded set of review hits against the code and return one verdict per hit. Use when a lens has already produced the hit and its evidence packet, and the job is to settle whether the scenario reaches a wrong outcome, on opus at medium effort because the packet narrows the read. ([bryzettler/dotfiles](https://github.com/bryzettler/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Checks a stated criterion against the real project state and reports PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after work is done, instead of grading it in the main thread. ([BTipFamily/HomeKin](https://github.com/BTipFamily/HomeKin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verify a change against what the project agreed — its ADRs and glossary in .about/, the Guidelines that fit the repo, and the acceptance criteria of the Ticket or PRD it serves — and report each departure with file:line. Run only when the user asks to verify a change, or through the /louie:verify Workflow, which runs one for each source. Not for hunting bugs, which a code review does, or for running the change, which Claude Code's /verify does; it changes nothing. - **verifier**: Verify completed work against its acceptance criteria and implementation evidence. ([fnodotdev/fno](https://github.com/fnodotdev/fno/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: workflow.md 6절 검증 표대로 변경 패키지의 lint·타입·테스트·빌드를 실행하고 결과를 보고하는 에이전트. "검증 돌려", "빌드·테스트 확인", PR 생성 전·리뷰 반영 후 재검증 시 사용. 코드를 고치지 않고 실패 원인을 파일:줄로 보고한다. ([fomalhaut84/myTangerine](https://github.com/fomalhaut84/myTangerine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Tries to disprove one hunter candidate from the code, or from a host's probe observations, and writes one vote file; dispatched by Rampart's audit workflow, not for direct use. ([FoodBreakPedro/rampart](https://github.com/FoodBreakPedro/rampart/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Run explicitly scoped checks and report their actual outcomes. ([formesean/skills](https://github.com/formesean/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Dream verification agent. Grades ONE finding with a clean context — confirms citations, attempts mechanical reproduction, runs the proof ladder (build, tests, characterization tests), and returns a verdict with confidence. Spawned by the dream run pipeline; not for general use. ([franccesco/dream](https://github.com/franccesco/dream/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independently proves or refutes a "done" claim with fresh eyes — runs the real flow, checks every requirement against observed evidence, hunts edge cases. Use before shipping significant work, after fixes, or whenever completion claims need independent verification. Never edits anything. ([franciszver/ways-of-working](https://github.com/franciszver/ways-of-working/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Verifies one review round's findings for the adversarial-review and improve skills. Give it the round's packets, then a filled-in verifier brief. ([frangkli/fstack](https://github.com/frangkli/fstack/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent verification agent — checks mathematical and scientific claims without seeing the working agent's reasoning ([frodo2647/triptych](https://github.com/frodo2647/triptych/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 実装を「完了」と判断した直後に 1 回だけ使う、読取専用の第三者照合。タスク仕様・設計・受け入れ条件に対して差分と検証結果を突き合わせ、正確性と要件に影響する gap だけを報告する。実装途中の都度確認や、自分の作業のダブルチェックには使わない。 ([froggugugugu/arin-term](https://github.com/froggugugugu/arin-term/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 3 copies)
- **verifier**: Read-only third-party conformance check, used once right after an implementation is judged complete. Compares the diff and verification results against the task spec, design, and acceptance criteria, and reports only the gaps that affect correctness or the stated requirements. Not for mid-implementation re-checks or for double-checking your own work. ([froggugugugu/project-blueprints](https://github.com/froggugugugu/project-blueprints/blob/HEAD/project-blueprint-en/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs reproduction commands from a review on the base and head commits in throwaway worktrees and reports which findings are real. Read-only. ([gabrielvieiraf/finitemonkey](https://github.com/gabrielvieiraf/finitemonkey/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the project to prove a change actually works. - **verifier**: Use to independently re-check a specific claim, ranking, or "X doesn't exist" conclusion — especially one produced by another agent (e.g. the researcher agent) — before it is written into a document. Do not use this agent to do open-ended research from scratch — give it a specific claim to check, not a general topic. - **verifier**: Checks a finished Ship Gate change before the session reports it done. Use after any change to the action, scripts, e2e, templates, tools or workflows. ([pauljosephdp/Ship-Gate](https://github.com/pauljosephdp/Ship-Gate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Menjalankan test, lint, E2E, check-layout, dan compare-text, lalu melaporkan PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED dengan bukti. Tidak memperbaiki kode atau mengubah test. ([pawangai/pawang-agents](https://github.com/pawangai/pawang-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: &gt; Goal-backward verification with mandatory fresh evidence. Never trusts memory or cached results. Edit freely. ([PeakyKeyman/Upstart-Skill-Share](https://github.com/PeakyKeyman/Upstart-Skill-Share/blob/HEAD/Cian%20McCann/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent check of a change before anyone calls it done. Give it Devesh's request word for word (when he gave none for this work, the message that started the session, or else the roadmap item's text, word for word), what was changed, and how the builder believes it can be seen working. It runs the product, looks for the result, and rules PASS, FAIL or CANNOT_VERIFY on the exact code in the worktree. It never fixes anything. Whenever product code changed, the done gate requires its PASS, or a CANNOT_VERIFY that names what only Devesh can provide. ([Peertopeer-solutions/revenue-os](https://github.com/Peertopeer-solutions/revenue-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Read-only Sonnet verifier for the generator-verifier loop. Never fixes anything itself. ([pengistudio/pengi-med-saas](https://github.com/pengistudio/pengi-med-saas/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Refute-first verification gate. Invoke after any attack-phase skill produces a CONFIRMED finding, before reporting. ([pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow](https://github.com/pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: The independent quality gate. Runs the project's tests and checks a work item against its PRD acceptance criteria, returning a clear PASS/FAIL with the failing-test signature. Phải là agent mới, không bao giờ là agent đã viết thay đổi. Trả về PASS, FAIL hoặc INCONCLUSIVE. Use proactively sau khi implementer báo xong và trước khi mở PR. - **verifier**: Independent verification agent. Use AFTER any workstream claims "done", before integration checkpoints, and before anything goes on a slide. - **verifier**: Use to decide independently whether a committed revision meets its spec. Writes checks from the spec before seeing code, runs everything, and returns one verdict (PASS, BLOCK or INCONCLUSIVE) with committed evidence and a checker approval. Can block; never fixes code. ([vibhortayal/agentic-team-dashboard](https://github.com/vibhortayal/agentic-team-dashboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Pre-completion verification agent. Runs tests, checks implementation against the original spec or plan, and reports pass/fail with evidence. Never fixes issues — only reports them. Use before declaring work complete. ([vincebrown/dotfiles](https://github.com/vincebrown/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Internal Shipyard specialist, invoked only by shipyard:coordinator or its saved workflows. The slice's test engineer: writes the test plan first (always as DRAFT), writes the tests from the requirements in the slice's test touch set, then commits them and produces executable evidence: scope check, canonical suite, planned validation tiers, routing facts, and an honest list of what was not verified. Never writes production code. ([vineethvarghese/shipyard](https://github.com/vineethvarghese/shipyard/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the gate and the app, then reports whether the change actually works. Use before any change is called done, because a green test suite and a working feature are different claims. ([VinnieRossi/DontTimeTheMarket](https://github.com/VinnieRossi/DontTimeTheMarket/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial verification agent. Runs the verify suite and targeted probes against a claimed-done change, and passes judgment only on pasted evidence. Use before declaring any non-trivial work complete. ([Vinylfigure/janus](https://github.com/Vinylfigure/janus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verifies that a change actually works by running the project's real checks and exercising real behavior. Use PROACTIVELY before declaring any task done. Reports evidence; does not fix code unless asked. ([vishalguptax/agent-engineering-kit](https://github.com/vishalguptax/agent-engineering-kit/blob/HEAD/.agent-kit/agents/verifier.md) · 3 copies)
- **verifier**: You test one factory job at runtime, one acceptance criterion at a time. You write exactly one file: `&lt;job folder&gt;/round-&lt;n&gt;/verify.md`, where `&lt;n&gt;` is the current round. You never edit code or any other file. ([vladtomescu/software-factory](https://github.com/vladtomescu/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 코딩 에이전트가 작성한 Flutter/Dart 코드가 작업 명세서의 요구사항을 충족하는지, 구현 계획의 시그니처를 준수했는지, 기존 프로젝트와 호환되는지를 검증한다. Flutter/Dart 코드 품질 검증은 flutter-reviewer 에이전트가 별도로 수행하며, 이 에이전트는 "명세대로 만들었는가"만 본다. PASS/FAIL 판정과 이슈 목록을 반환한다. 코드를 직접 수정하지 않는 읽기 전용 에이전트다. ([voidreader/band-of-mercenaries](https://github.com/voidreader/band-of-mercenaries/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial verifier. Independently confirms or REFUTES a claimed finding or fix in a fresh context: re-open cited lines, re-run tests, re-query data, re-read the diff. Never fixes; returns an evidence-backed verdict. ([w159/atlas-connectwise](https://github.com/w159/atlas-connectwise/blob/HEAD/plugins/atlas/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 작업 완료 후 실제로 동작하는지 검증합니다. 기능 구현 완료, 리팩토링 후 회귀 확인, 커밋/PR 전 검증이 필요할 때 사용합니다. ([wannysim/mumak-www](https://github.com/wannysim/mumak-www/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Fresh-context 驗收員。第 2 級產出（論文正文與文獻筆記、公開網站內容、規則檔與 skill、評估報告的結論與出處、對外主張）以及它們修完後的複驗，交給它逐條核對驗收條件；第 0、1 級不派它，分級見 `.claude/rules/dispatch-and-verify.md`「驗證不自驗」。照指示不改檔；唯讀只靠指示，它有 Bash，技術上寫得了檔。派工時只給驗收條件與檔案路徑，不要給原始任務的推理過程。 ([wayhong0928/claude-workflow-template](https://github.com/wayhong0928/claude-workflow-template/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 验证员（轻量档）：在明确安全边界内核查交付声明与物理可执行真实性。当前仅有 Bash 时只做 SAFE_STATIC；仓库脚本、构建、测试、安装和服务须有已证明的隔离容器或沙箱，否则记未验证。只报告不改源码。 ([wdfdsyhb/zcode-plugins-community](https://github.com/wdfdsyhb/zcode-plugins-community/blob/HEAD/plugins/zcode_skills/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Differential and adversarial verification of a port against fixtures recorded from the original. - **verifier**: Checks assigned acceptance criteria against the built change, from check output the main session provides and by reading the code, and reports each one as verified, failed or pending, with how. - **verifier**: You confirm a milestone is done. Prefer automated probes; spot-check code for false greens. ([WisecraftAI/Zero](https://github.com/WisecraftAI/Zero/blob/HEAD/support/agent-workflow/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Independent Acceptance Verifier. - **verifier-adversarial**: Adversarial verifier inspired by Devin's Planner/Actor/Verifier pattern. - **verifier-agent**: You verify one canonical finding. - **verifier-agent**: Independent verifier for an already-raised finding. Re-reads the cited location cold and returns one structured verdict. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/blob/HEAD/evals/loop/modules/audit-plugin/benchmarks/dev/plugin-1/tree/agents/verifier-agent.md) · ★8)
- **verifier-agent**: Calls a task-pipeline node's agent surface through a real client. Runs the proving call and the contract tests, compares the served snapshot with the contract source, and probes typed errors, scope refusal and idempotent retry. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-agent.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-agent**: Activar en el Loop para ejecutar las capas no funcionales del Completion Map (static, security, arch) y reportar PASS/FAIL con evidencia, sin modificar el contrato ni el mapa. - **verifier-agent**: Use after the builder-agent completes any non-trivial implementation. Executes the story's Acceptance Check Manifest, reviews changes for correctness/safety/consistency, and returns an evidence-gated verdict. Always invoke after auth-related or database changes. ([atimmer418/FRED](https://github.com/atimmer418/FRED/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: Compliance and traceability agent for the parish app SDLC. Confirms BA requirements are implemented in code and validated by tests, producing a requirement-to-code-to-test traceability matrix. Use when .claude/memory.md shows the current phase as "verifier". Read-only over code — does not modify implementation or tests. ([Catholic-Parish-St-Luke-s-Marsden-Park/st_luke_parish_app](https://github.com/Catholic-Parish-St-Luke-s-Marsden-Park/st_luke_parish_app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: **Version**: 1.0.0 **Phase**: 3 (Verification) **Predecessor**: Implementer Agent (Phase 2) **Successor**: Proof Agent (Phase 4) ([griff843/unit-talk-production](https://github.com/griff843/unit-talk-production/blob/HEAD/governance/claude-os/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: Definition-of-ready verifier subagent (ticket-refine spec 5.1 step 7, "Verify") - independently checks a synthesized refined ticket against the definition-of-ready checklist before ticket-refine reports a passing verdict. ([JustinChan-fd/dev-loop](https://github.com/JustinChan-fd/dev-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier-agent**: **Role**: Defensive skeptic who validates findings without killing them prematurely. Uses BLOCK gates, not KILL gates. ([Mohammedkamal90/Trackator-team](https://github.com/Mohammedkamal90/Trackator-team/blob/HEAD/redteam-trackator/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: Judge one ossify work item at close, in a context that did not build it: Layer 3 (documented patterns), Layer 4 (fidelity, pattern, absence) and the mutation check, per `skills/close/references/impl-check.md` §4, §4b and §4c under the ossify plugin root. Dispatched by work-item close only. Returns one JSON object; never writes verify.md, never stages, never commits. ([pulseai-labs/pulsestack](https://github.com/pulseai-labs/pulsestack/blob/HEAD/ossify/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: 發佈前的把關者。逐條比對會議總結的每個主張是否有逐字稿/投影片來源支撐，標記幻覺、遺漏重點、術語不一致，回傳 pass/fail 與逐條修正建議。當總結完成、需要「驗證/查核/verify」才放行時使用。 ([s96cax98leo/meeting-harness](https://github.com/s96cax98leo/meeting-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-agent**: Nexus Verifier Agent (DTX 495): evaluates a student's practice response against the reference solution and the confirmed prerequisite record — technical correctness, source grounding, citation accuracy, consistency with the confirmed prerequisite, independence/evidence validity (forced from the independence record, never inferred from writing style), academic-integrity recording, and whether the evidence supports a learning-improvement claim (gated on evidence validity, exposed via overall.claim_eligibility). Use after the student submits a practice response, before the final test report. ([tjppppppppp/-dtx-495](https://github.com/tjppppppppp/-dtx-495/blob/HEAD/nexus-agent-system/.opencode/agents/verifier-agent.md))
- **verifier-b**: First-principles verification of pipeline output with NO Decision Playbook access. Works from source inspection and web evidence only. Use during EVALUATE phase for N-version verification alongside Verifier A. ([rayanino/kr](https://github.com/rayanino/kr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-b.md))
- **verifier-bias**: You are a bias and reasoning auditor. Your job is to find systematic errors in how the author thinks — the patterns of flawed reasoning, not missing information. ([linfangyu42/agentic-audit](https://github.com/linfangyu42/agentic-audit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-bias.md))
- **verifier-blackhat**: Read-only independent Verifier of final security-diff coverage and Attacker reports; recommends Main-owned retries, never dispatches or reproduces exploits. ([letwir/config](https://github.com/letwir/config/blob/HEAD/.HARNESS/agents/verifier-blackhat.md) · ★1)
- **verifier-builder**: Use when implementing or debugging a Caliper verifier (V1 range, V2 cross-SKU consistency, V3 cross-source, V4 round-trip, V5 adversarial critic), including abstention thresholds and human-readable verdict reasons. ([HERPESME/caliper](https://github.com/HERPESME/caliper/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-builder.md))
- **verifier-deep**: Gate 2 verifier for judgment calls: root cause vs symptom, semantic equivalence, edge-case coverage, security/correctness-critical output. Criteria carry IDs C1..Cn; the review is pinned to a diff range. ([midego1/claude-orchestrate](https://github.com/midego1/claude-orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-deep.md) · ★10)
- **verifier-deep**: Adversarial verification designer for criteria where a false "it works" is expensive: authorization and data scoping, invariants over money or scheduling, consent and audit trails, and anything asserted by calling an API directly. It designs the probe plan most likely to break the rule and runs nothing. Hand the plan to verifier-std to execute. ([santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit](https://github.com/santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-deep.md) · ★3)
- **verifier-defenses**: Evaluates whether existing sanitizers, validation schemas, or security barriers neutralize the candidate. ([arcobaleno64/three-lens](https://github.com/arcobaleno64/three-lens/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-defenses.md))
- **verifier-depth**: You are a depth and completeness auditor. Your job is to find what the author didn't say — the missing dimensions, not the wrong ones. ([linfangyu42/agentic-audit](https://github.com/linfangyu42/agentic-audit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-depth.md))
- **verifier-deterministic**: Consolidated deterministic verification agent. Executes all tool-driven checks (gitleaks, ruff, pip-audit, pytest) and reports structured results. Dispatched by ai-verify before LLM judgment agents. ([arcasilesgroup/claudeline](https://github.com/arcasilesgroup/claudeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-deterministic.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-e2e**: Walks a finished vertical slice in a real browser, as a person would, hunting the defects a green suite cannot see: dead ends, gestures that do nothing, screens that render but say nothing true, states nobody can reach. Pick it when a whole feature is claimed done and someone is about to click it. Use verifier-std when the probes are already written and only need running. ([santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit](https://github.com/santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-e2e.md) · ★3)
- **verifier-editor**: You turn five analysts' parts into one deck a reader can trust. You check the facts, make the parts agree, write the summary, the business quality scorecard and the thesis, and hold every slide to the writing rules. You own sections 0.1, 1.12 and 5.1. Agent name for phase.py: `verifier-editor`. ([geo0apostolakis/stock-analysis-teardown](https://github.com/geo0apostolakis/stock-analysis-teardown/blob/HEAD/skills/stock-analysis-teardown/agents/verifier-editor.md) · ★1)
- **verifier-engineer**: Use for the constraint ledger, the executable self-check gate and the independent verifier - the parts that raise the hard-constraint pass rate. ([hkrishna42/ple-harness](https://github.com/hkrishna42/ple-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-engineer.md))
- **verifier-fact**: You are a factual accuracy auditor. Your job is to verify the truth status of claims — distinguishing what's confirmed from what's inferred, uncertain, or wrong. ([linfangyu42/agentic-audit](https://github.com/linfangyu42/agentic-audit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-fact.md))
- **verifier-fast**: Gate 2 verifier for criteria-checkable output. Compares output against explicit done-criteria (IDs C1..Cn), pinned to a diff range. ([midego1/claude-orchestrate](https://github.com/midego1/claude-orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-fast.md) · ★10)
- **verifier-high**: verifier at effort high (same instructions, read from verifier.md). Pick for nothing-lost diff reviews and long checklists; the Agent tool's effort param does not pin effort, this file does. ([yarinsa/.claude](https://github.com/yarinsa/.claude/blob/HEAD/orchestrator-kit/agents/verifier-high.md))
- **verifier-hm**: Post-change verification (Haiku, medium). Runs the build, the relevant tests and checks, and returns only the result (pass/fail plus an error summary). Not for: fixing what fails (developer-sm and up). Does not edit code. ([Karasoy-Teknoloji/agent-crew-kit](https://github.com/Karasoy-Teknoloji/agent-crew-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-hm.md))
- **verifier-impact**: Calibrates the authentic blast radius, privilege boundary violation, and CVSS vector. ([arcobaleno64/three-lens](https://github.com/arcobaleno64/three-lens/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-impact.md))
- **verifier-integrity-hunter**: Reward-hack detection specialist — looks for test hardcoding, verifier bypass, require-tests evasion, and quiet requirement-narrowing. Takes the diff AND the run's trajectory (verdict LOG, TDD red→green history) as input, not just the final diff. Use before opening a PR, alongside code-reviewer/test-hunter, or whenever a run's own self-report of "tests pass now" needs independent scrutiny. ([reach0908/paul-loop](https://github.com/reach0908/paul-loop/blob/HEAD/tools/ship-flow/agents/verifier-integrity-hunter.md))
- **verifier-judge-agent**: Stage 4c adversarial judge. Runs on a DIFFERENT model from the extractor; a different provider is opt-in. ([ColdMountain24/lit2db-marketplace](https://github.com/ColdMountain24/lit2db-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/lit2db/agents/verifier-judge-agent.md))
- **verifier-L1**: verifier at level L1: opus low. Same role as the verifier agent. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/verifier-L1.md))
- **verifier-L2**: verifier at level L2: sonnet high. Same role as the verifier agent. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/verifier-L2.md))
- **verifier-L3**: verifier at level L3: fable xhigh. Same role as the verifier agent. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/verifier-L3.md))
- **verifier-light**: Mechanical verification agent for a single check with an exact procedure: run one command and report its output, query one value, hit one endpoint, check one log for a pattern. Use verifier-std when the check requires interpreting the result. ([santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit](https://github.com/santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-light.md) · ★3)
- **verifier-packager**: Package the verifiers for a browser task — freeze the suite, extract the LLM-judge contract and gold fast path, specify exactly what cannot be shipped, and test whether any of it is actually executable. Use when packaging or auditing the scoring half of a browser-task delivery. ([deccanai-org/browser-gym](https://github.com/deccanai-org/browser-gym/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-packager.md) · 4 copies)
- **verifier-product**: Reads the documentation, scenarios, user-facing strings and neighbouring features around a task-pipeline node and reports whether the change leaves a documented or user-facing behaviour false or collides with another feature. Returns an eight-key tier report for graph.py certify. Use as the product tier, a blind reading, when a node claims to be finished. Not for line-level correctness (the unit tier) or call graphs (the seam tier). ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-product.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-prompt**: You verify that a Drupal fix actually works by running concrete checks. ([AJV009/drupal-contrib-workbench](https://github.com/AJV009/drupal-contrib-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/drupal-contribute-fix/agents/verifier-prompt.md) · ★4)
